### Page 1

Taiwan Consumer Discretionary

What’s new: We recently received an update from management. 
Management maintains its target of gaining market share in the US, with 
potential to increase its coverage with a more vertically integrated model, 
including manufacturing and sales channels, to enhance its profitability. We 
remain positive on the stock due to its leading industry position and see 
higher market-share gain potential, on top of its solid execution capability 
vs. global peers, over the long term. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call.

What’s the impact: Offering 2026 guidance. Nien Made targets to grow 
its custom-made revenue in the US by mid-single digit % YoY, custom-
made in non-US markets by high-single digit % YoY and ready-made 
revenue by low-single digit % YoY in 2026. Management is confident that 
2026 will deliver YoY revenue growth, despite 5M26 revenue falling 0.3% 
YoY. Management expects revenue momentum to accelerate momentum 
from June 2026, from the ramping up of new capacity.

Solid production execution to overcome macro uncertainty and gain 
share. Despite facing tariffs (vs. the current US tariff of 17.5%) since 2Q25, 
Nien Made has improved its gross margin since 2021, mainly due to efforts 
in manufacturing, retail, lean production and brand value. Nien Made 
implemented a price hike in February 2026, but still with 30-40% price gap 
vs. its key competitor, thus the company expects its market share gain 
story in the US to continue. The company will continue to work on the 
balance of share gains and profitability. Its phase-one construction has 
been delayed to September 2026, aiming to hire 1,000 persons vs. 1,700 
headcount in Mexico and 4,200 headcount in Cambodia. Nien Made also 
uses a D2C model (Veneta.com) in European markets (ie, the Netherlands, 
Denmark and Belgium), which is one of the factors for its operating margin 
expansion. Besides, the company sees limited impact on gross margin 
from the PVC price hike. We trim our 2026-27E EPS by 1-4%, mainly on 
our more aggressive opex assumptions. We introduce our 2028 forecasts.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call on Nien Made with an 
unchanged 12M TP of TWD515, still based on a target PER of 20x on our 
1-year forward EPS vs. its past 3-year PER trading range of 11-21x. Key 
downside risks: lower-than-expected revenue from custom-made products 
and macro/tariff uncertainties.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 1-6% below Bloomberg consensus, 
likely as we are slightly more conservative on its operating margins.

24 June 2026

Intact 2026 outlook

➢ Aiming for market-share gains in the following years 
➢ Revenue momentum should heat up in 2H26 on capacity expansion 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; with an unchanged 12M TP of TWD515

Company profile 
Founded in July 2007 and listed on Taiwan’s main board in December 2015, Nien Made Enterprise 
(Nien Made) produces window coverings such as venetian blinds, shutters and shades (ie, cellular 
shades, roller blinds and roman blinds). Its major clients include dealers, designers and small specialty 
stores as well as big box retailers (ie, Home Depot and Wal-Mart).

market, supported by a sharp rebound in Meta share (Minipack 3N ramp) and

• Amazon ASIC engine reignites, TAM also broadening. After a transition 
period in 1H26, Accton’s AWS business is set to rebound in 2H26, with AWS

procurement. Overall, we see AWS-related TAM expanding beyond modules 
into networking and server integration, providing a robust growth engine even 
as competition intensifies. 
• Server rack expansion unlocks scenario upside. Accton is leveraging its 
switch design expertise to enter the server rack market, targeting both generic 
and AI-optimized server opportunities. Our scenario analysis suggests that 
every 1% market share gain in U.S. CSP general servers could drive ~9% of 
incremental revenue (2025 base), with further upside from optical 
transceivers, liquid cooling, and scale -up/scale-across switch adjacencies. 
Management expects initial server revenue contribution in 2H26, and we 
believe this vertical integration strategy positions Accton to capture a larger 
share of the datacenter value chain as cloud architectures evolve.

Summary Investment Thesis and Valuation  
Investment Thesis 
Accton stock trades around the high end of its historical 
valuation range, reflecting its strong earnings outlook, high 
ROE, and expanding TAM. We expect an accelerating earnings 
CAGR over 2025 –28 for Accton, driven by rapid 800G/1.6T 
switch upgrades, share gains in Meta’s ToR, and a resumption of 
AWS ASIC momentum. TAM expansion into server racks and 
optical adjacency offers further upside optionality. OW. 
Valuation 
Our Jun-27 PT of NT$3,600 is based on 12M fwd EPS, at the 
higher end of historical up -cycle valuations, given the 
accelerating datacenter whitebox switch upgrade cycle, secular 
AI demand, and potential re-rating opportunity from the optical 
biz.

Initiate at OW with Jun-27 PT of NT$3,600 
We initiate coverage of Accton with an OW rating and Jun-27 PT of NT$3,600 (around 
~26x 12M fwd EPS, at the higher end of mid-cycle valuations). We are positive on 
Accton due to 1) resumed AWS ASIC momentum in 2H26; 2) 800G/1.6T datacenter 
switch upgrades with a multi-year AI-driven accelerating upgrade trend; 3) new 800G 
switch project ramp; and 4) AI networking TAM expansion on rising Ethernet adoption 
in the AI scale-up fabric. Consequently, we forecast 44%/51% revenue/earnings CAGRs 
over 2025-28.

Key downside risks include: 1) faster-than-expected market share loss in AWS AI ASIC 
module; 2) slower-than-expected 800G/1.6T networking upgrade trend; 3) weaker-than-
expected AWS ASIC demand; and 4) larger-than-expected margin dilution during 
vertical integration.

JPMe above consensus estimates on accelerating datacenter 
switch upgrade cycle and positive view on AWS Trn 3 
momentum 
We believe consensus is underestimating both (1) AI ASIC demand and (2) 
the 800G/1.6T data center switch refresh cycle. Our industry research suggests AWS’s 
AI ASIC momentum is likely to remain strong into next year. In data center switching, 
we expect the 800G upgrade cycle to accelerate across leading hyperscalers beginning 
in 2H25, with an initial ramp of 1.6T in 2H26, supported by Nvidia’s 800G/1.6T 
networking adoption roadmap.

We expect the super general server upcycle and secular AI server demand to drive the 
traditional datacenter Ethernet ODM switch demand in the next few years. In addition to 
the traditional Ethernet fabric, AI compute fabric will create an incremental switch TAM 
for Accton as CSPs rapidly increase Ethernet adoption.

AI compute fabric will accelerate the 800G/1.6T upgrade cycle, which could even 
become a bigger market vs. the traditional one, on our estimates. Our research also 
indicates Accton has secured a new 800G program at a key U.S. hyperscaler, which 
implies that Accton will even outgrow the fast-growing market. There could be potential 
upside for Accton’s networking biz from increased penetration in scale-up switching at a 
key ASIC customer.

Overall, the combination of strong top-line momentum and operating leverage drives 
our 2026/27 earnings estimates to be ~12%/6% above consensus estimates.

Overview of historical stock price and stock catalysts 
Revenue momentum has been the key driver of Accton’s share price. Since listing in 
1995, Accton’s revenue has increased by more than 50x, while GM has generally 
remained within the 10–20% range. Over the same period, the stock has appreciated by 
more than 90x.

Accton traded in a prolonged range during 2003–2014, reflecting cyclical networking 
demand. In 2H15, revenue momentum began to accelerate as the company became a 
JDM (Joint Development Manufacturer) partner to key CSP (Cloud Service Provider) 
customers, enabling it to break into the data center white-box switch market.

Revenue growth re-accelerated to a 22% CAGR in 2015–2019 (vs. 2% in 2002–2015), 
and GM recovered from a trough of 15% in 2014 to ~20% in 2019. We attribute this 
inflection to the structural adoption of data center white-box switches. Notably, 
Accton’s stock rose 17.5x over this period (vs. +52% for TAIEX).

Momentum paused in 2020–1H23 due to the networking chip shortage and a mix shift 
in Meta’s 400G switch program, but re-accelerated in 2H23 on strong Amazon AI ASIC 
demand and rising expectations for AI-driven networking upgrades over the medium to 
longer term.

The stock corrected in 9M24 amid a networking destocking cycle and Gaudi demand 
downside, but reached a new high in 4Q24 on stronger-than-expected Amazon ASIC 
demand and an improving networking upgrade outlook.

In April 2025, shares sold off on rising macro uncertainty tied to Trump’s trade policy, 
before a V-shaped rebound to new highs supported by tariff exemptions and robust AWS 
AI ASIC and networking demand.

Shares traded range-bound in 2H25 on concerns around the AWS transition, but have 
continued to break records since late 2025 driven by better-than-expected revenue 
momentum, aided by a smoother-than-expected AWS ASIC transition, potential AWS 
ASIC demand upside, and a new 800G customer ramp.

Looking ahead, we expect more sustained stock momentum into next year on resilient 
AWS AI ASIC demand and 800G/1.6T switch upgrade cycles.

Shortened networking upgrade cadence and expanding TAM 
to sustain a premium P/E 
Accton’s valuation has re-rated meaningfully (from 5–10x in 2015 to 20–30x today), as 
it gained exposure to the data center white-box switch market. The multiple peaked in 
1H21 at over +2x standard deviation (or higher) on anticipation of the 400G cycle ramp, 
before de-rating as the company lost share to a key U.S. competitor in the 400G 
generation.

Looking forward, we expect earnings momentum to further accelerate with potential 
earnings upside in the coming months. We forecast a ~50% earnings CAGR in 2025-28

We expect Accton to outgrow its major peers (JPMe 50%+ earnings CAGR in 2025-27 
vs. industry median of 25-30%), driven by an accelerating datacenter whitebox switch 
upgrade cycle, market share gains in a key U.S. CSP 800G project, and secular ASIC 
demand. Therefore, we expect Accton stock to continue trading at a premium multiple 
versus peers averaging at ~25-30x.

In addition, the stocks of Accton’s key industry peers, Celestica and Arista, have traded 
at a 20-30% premium despite slower earnings momentum. There could be a potential re-
rating opportunity for Accton to the 25-30x range, supported by stronger earnings power 
and its differentiated vertical integration—from networking cards, data center switch 
systems, and optical components, to AI chip modules and potentially server systems.

Accton also screens with the highest ROE among peers, reflecting efficient capital 
deployment and rapid earnings growth. Overall, our Jun-27 PT of NT$3,600 is based on 
~26x 12M fwd EPS and implies a valuation above mid-cycle.

I. Network switches - the next key driver for 
Accton 
We see network switches as the next key growth driver for Accton. Switch revenue 
growth has accelerated over the past 4–6 quarters, led by the ramp in high-end platforms 
(400G/800G). Demand for high-end data center switches is being pulled forward by AI 
—both the build-out of AI networking fabrics and the incremental expansion of existing 
Ethernet networks as AI-inference-driven traditional/ASIC server deployments scale 
(we estimate data center switching demand at ~15–20% CAGR in 2020–2024).

Our industry research also suggests Accton has been gaining m/s in Meta’s 800G 
program since late 2025. Management indicated that 800G revenue has reached low-
teens % of total revenue in 1Q26 (up from high single digits in 4Q25), driven by a new 
800G customer ramp, and we expect momentum to build further over the next few 
quarters. We believe a multi-year server upcycle and an accelerating switch upgrade 
cycle should sustain strong growth in Accton’s switch business. Overall, we forecast 
Accton to outgrow the white-box switch market, with a 2025–28 switch revenue CAGR 
of ~90% versus an industry TAM CAGR of ~75%, driven by continued market share 
gains.

Of note, our TAM does not include scale-up fabrics, AMD’s GPU related switch TAM, 
and AI ASIC switch TAM other than AWS. There could also be upside to our TAM 
estimates from enriching Nvidia’s product offering (i.e. Nvidia’s “extreme co-design” 
Rubin platform). LPX/CMX/Vera CPU standalone racks will likely create additional 
ToR switch demand.

Accelerating hyperscaler server spending driving datacenter 
switch demand 
Leading hyperscalers have been stepping up data center investment since 2H23, with AI 
infrastructure a clear priority. Our U.S. hardware analyst, Samik Chatterjee, raised the 
aggregate cloud capex for the top four U.S. hyperscalers to +80% YoY growth in 2026

(vs. prior +63% YoY) post CSPs’ recent capex guidance raises, following strong growth

Data center infrastructure spending extends well beyond servers to include networking, 
data center shells, utilities, and other components. While switches represent only ~5-
10% of total data center hardware spend, they are critical to connectivity, security, and 
overall data center efficiency. As AI compute scales and cluster sizes expand, bandwidth 
requirements and switch capacity needs rise sharply to support higher-throughput data 
transmission.

Over the past 2–3 years, CSPs have prioritized AI server investment and data center 
build-outs, while networking has lagged. Gartner® data shows ODM-direct server 
spending increased 207%/127% in 2024/2025, versus ODM-direct switch spending 
growth of 18%/103% over the same period. We expect CSPs to step up networking 
spending to reduce latency and improve data transmission rates. Overall, we believe 
aggressive AI server investment will pull forward a multi-year networking switch 
upgrade cycle—supportive for white-box switch vendors such as Accton.

AI-driven general-purpose server spending drives demand for 
generic networking fabric investments 
• We introduce our proprietary, bottom-up analysis of Accton’s generic white-box 
Ethernet switch TAM. Given Accton’s focus on ToR switches for U.S. hyperscalers, 
our framework centers on projected demand for generic white-box ToR switches at 
the top 4 U.S. CSPs. We also include the front-end AI server network in our 
forecast. Overall, we forecast a ~43% CAGR over 2025–28 for the general-purpose 
white-box Ethernet ToR switch revenue TAM, driven by (1) stronger general-
purpose server demand, (2) rapid build-out of AI front-end network fabrics, and (3) 
ASP uplift from ongoing speed migration. Key assumptions include: 
• Top 4 U.S. CSP server shipments: Historical server motherboard shipments are 
based on ODM feedback. We expect shipments to accelerate over the next few years 
on AI-driven inference demand, and estimate shipment growth of 40%/25%/20% in 
2026/27/28. There could be upside risks to our general server shipment assumptions 
driven by fast-growing agentic AI demand. 
• NICs per server: General-purpose servers typically have 2–4 NICs. We expect 
NICs per server to gradually trend higher as bandwidth requirements increase with 
AI inference workloads. 
• Ports per NIC: NICs typically carry 1–2 ports. We assume 2 ports per NIC in our 
analysis. 
• Oversubscription ratio: In a leaf-spine topology, ToR switches connect to server-
facing NIC ports (downlinks) and to spine switches (uplinks). In traditional IT 
spine-leaf fabrics, the downlink-to-uplink bandwidth ratio is typically below 3:1. We 
expect this ratio to compress over time as AI inference workloads increase and 
uplink bandwidth requirements rise. 
• White-box switch penetration: White-box Ethernet adoption in data center ToR 
switches has been increasing, supported by better TCO (Total Cost of Ownership) 
and a more mature open NOS (Network Operating Software) ecosystem. 
• Port shipment mix by speed: While 400G/800G ports are being adopted in AI 
server front-end networks (initially in 2023/2026, respectively), 100G should remain 
the mainstream choice for the broader, generic networking fabric.

11

### Page 12

Albert Hung AC 
(886-2) 2725-9875 
albert.hung@jpmchase.com 
Asia Pacific Equity Research 
25 June 2026

• ASP per port by speed: Our port ASP assumptions are anchored in our 650 Group 
historical data and our U.S. team forecasts. While we expect price erosion at a given 
speed, we forecast a 6% CAGR in blended ASPs over 2025–28, driven by a faster 
upgrade cycle.

AI/ML drives both front-end and back-end networking 
upgrades 
AI data centers typically run two distinct networks: a front-end network (traditional 
Ethernet fabric that connects to the external network/Internet) and a back-end network 
(cluster fabric that connects the AI training pods) (see Figure 12, illustration of front-
end and back-end networks in AI/ML clusters).

We believe AI is creating an incremental back-end GPU training network while also 
pulling forward refresh cycles in the front-end. We expect back-end to upgrade faster, 
reflecting the roughly annual cadence of the GPU platform cycle. At the same time, we

12

### Page 13

Albert Hung AC 
(886-2) 2725-9875 
albert.hung@jpmchase.com 
Asia Pacific Equity Research 
25 June 2026

expect ongoing upgrades in the traditional front-end network as training and inference 
drive materially higher bandwidth requirements and overall data volumes.

Arista management has suggested that every dollar of back-end network spend by data 
center customers can drive an additional dollar of similar spend on the front-end 
network—implying that the front-end also scales with the growing size of AI clusters. 
We also see continued spec upgrades in front-end NICs (e.g., BlueField 3 400G in 
GB200/300 moving to BlueField 4 800G in VR200 systems). Our research indicates 
that one key U.S. hyperscaler has also upgraded its in-house front-end NIC solution for 
NVL72 servers from 200G in GB200/300 to 400G in VR200.

Beyond AI servers, we are also seeing a multi-year general-purpose server upcycle 
driven by rising agentic-AI workloads, which should further support traditional front-
end networking demand.

Ethernet networking vendors (e.g., Arista, Juniper, Cisco, Accton) have served the front-
end networking market for decades and should benefit from the AI-driven upgrade cycle 
(particularly white-box data center switch vendors). While AI GPU back-end networks 
have historically skewed toward InfiniBand, we see Ethernet increasingly penetrating 
AI clusters, creating incremental TAM for Ethernet switch vendors. Our research 
suggests that several of the top four U.S. hyperscalers (e.g., Amazon, Google) are 
inclined to deploy Ethernet in their back-end networks as well.

Figure 12: Illustration of the front-end and back-end networks in AI/ML clusters

Source: Cisco.

There are two types of GPU networks: AI scale-up and AI scale-out. Today, Accton is 
primarily exposed to GPU scale-out networks through a key CSP customer, and we 
believe it is gaining share at an additional U.S. hyperscaler. While Nvidia’s GPU servers 
are likely to remain anchored to proprietary NVLink for scale-up connectivity, we 
believe AMD platforms and CSP in-house ASIC servers are more open to non-Nvidia 
scale-up options, including Ethernet-based approaches (e.g., ESUN/SUE), UALink, and

13

### Page 14

Albert Hung AC 
(886-2) 2725-9875 
albert.hung@jpmchase.com 
Asia Pacific Equity Research 
25 June 2026

PCIe-based solutions.

We are also seeing early signs of Ethernet-based scale-up network development, notably 
OCP’s ESUN and Broadcom’s SUE (Scale-up Ethernet). If Ethernet-based scale-up 
switching is adopted in next-generation AI server architectures, it could open a 
meaningful incremental TAM opportunity for Accton. Our research indicates Accton is 
already engaged with a key ASIC customer on scale-up switch design work.

Smart Network Interface Card (Smart 
NIC) 
A smart NIC is a programmable 
accelerator equipped with computing 
power that boosts datacenter networking, 
security, and storage functions by 
offloading non-compute centric tasks 
from the CPU. There are different types 
of smart NICs such as ASIC, FPGA, and 
SoC. 
Higher GPU bandwidth drives back-end scale-out switch demand 
A network interface card (NIC) connects an end device (e.g., storage, GPU, and CPU 
servers) to the compute network fabric. NIC-to-switch link speed is a key trigger for 
upgrades across the switching stack—from ToR/leaf to spine. In practice, datacenter 
operators aim to align NIC/switch and switch/switch speeds at defined “match points” to 
optimize throughput, latency, and overall network efficiency. Upgrade timing is also 
shaped by total cost of ownership and ecosystem readiness (e.g., availability and 
maturity of switch ICs, SerDes, and optical transceivers). When speeds are mismatched, 
networks can experience higher latency, inefficient utilization, and ultimately 
suboptimal performance.

NIC specifications also influence protocol choices (e.g., InfiniBand vs. Ethernet). 
Nvidia’s ConnectX SuperNICs support both Ethernet and InfiniBand, while most CSP 
in-house networking solutions are Ethernet-based rather than InfiniBand.

Table 4: Key components of a datacenter leaf-spine networking system

Networking equipment/devices Functions  
Network interface card (NIC) Gateways between server nodes and networks  
Leaf switch/top-of-rack switch First connections from NICs to the rest of networks 
Spine switch Responsible for the east-west traffic. The port capacity of spine switches determines the scale/number of racks 
Connects different devices in networks. There are copper or optical cables. The former has cheaper cost and consumes less 
Cable power, while the latter could connects the devices with better connecting efficiency in long distance. Usually copper cable is 
used within rack, while optical cable is used between racks.  
Optical transceiver Allows longer connecting distance between leaf-to-spine switches

Source: Company data. 
As accelerator chips become more compute-intensive and memory bandwidth increases, 
we expect NIC specifications to step up (e.g., higher link speeds and more advanced 
feature sets) and/or NIC volumes per server to rise. For example, Nvidia’s A100/H100/ 
H200 platforms have moved to 400G SmartNICs (ConnectX-7), versus 100G SmartNIC 
configurations used in P100/V100-era deployments circa 2016.

While initial Blackwell server configurations continued to use 400G ConnectX-7 (CX7) 
NICs as networking adapters, largely reflecting time-to-market priorities, Nvidia’s 800G 
SmartNIC (ConnectX-8; CX8) reached general availability in 2H25 and GB300 systems 
have shifted to CX8. In our view, this transition—alongside a smoother GB200-to-
GB300 ramp driven by the board redesign and strong GB300 demand this year—should 
expedite the 800G switch upgrade cycle.

Looking ahead, we expect Nvidia and AMD to maintain a short GPU roadmap cadence 
to meet surging AI compute demand—an important catalyst for continued networking 
switch upgrades. Nvidia’s next-generation GPUs (i.e., Vera Rubin servers) are expected 
to adopt 1.6T-class NICs (e.g., ConnectX-9). In addition, our research suggests that the 
NIC-to-GPU ratio doubles in VR200 systems to 2:1 (vs. 1:1 in GB200/300), with 
potential for a further step-up in the Vera Rubin Ultra.

A faster switch upgrade cycle—paired with the exponential growth in GPU memory 
bandwidth—supports a materially expanding AI switch TAM. We believe Accton is 
well positioned to benefit from the sustained secular demand in GPU scale-out 
networks.

Quantifying Nvidia’s AI/ML ToR TAM for Accton 
In the analysis below, we size the AI-related high-speed switch TAM and assess the 
associated growth opportunity for Accton. Given Nvidia’s dominant position in the AI 
data center GPU market, we anchor our framework on Nvidia-led AI infrastructure and 
estimate port demand based on our Nvidia GPU system forecasts and bandwidth

specifications.

We see two primary drivers for the GPU scale-out switch TAM: exponential growth in 
GPU networking bandwidth and sustained strength in AI demand. To support rapidly 
scaling AI workloads, GPU bandwidth has stepped up from 400G in Hopper HGX/ 
GB200-class platforms to 800G in Blackwell/Rubin HGX/GB300. Our research further 
suggests that GPU bandwidth could double to 1.6T in VR200 and then double again in 
the Rubin Ultra generation.

For ASP, our 800G switch port assumptions are based on our BoM cost estimates (see 
Table 9). On this basis, we estimate Nvidia’s high-speed ToR switch revenue TAM for 
the back-end GPU network at ~US$2.6bn in 2025. We expect this TAM to expand at a 
126% CAGR over 2025–28, driven by the step-function increase in GPU bandwidth and 
sustained AI demand.

We see even stronger growth potential for Nvidia’s GPU networking Ethernet switch 
TAM as Ethernet continues to gain share versus InfiniBand. Dell’Oro estimates Ethernet 
penetration in AI back-end networks increased from ~20% in 2023 to 60%+ by the end 
of 2025. We expect Ethernet adoption to continue rising over the next few years within 
Nvidia’s GPU back-end networks, and forecast a ~164% CAGR for the Ethernet switch 
TAM over 2025–28.

Accelerating network upgrade cycles driven by AI compute 
Gartner® data points to a rapid pace of speed migration in data center switching, driven 
by surging data generation and rising workload complexity—particularly with the 
emergence of gen-AI—while enterprise customers continue to upgrade at a much slower 
pace. Mainstream enterprise switch ports remain anchored at 10G (90%+ of campus 
switch port shipments in 2025). In contrast, for datacenter customers, sub-10G ports 
have fallen to ~15% of total switch port shipments from 90%+ in 2015, while ports 
above 100G have climbed to 50%+ versus near-zero in 2015.

Data center customers kicked off the 100G upgrade cycle in 2015, and the transition 
from 100G to 200/400G took roughly seven years. By comparison, the 400G-to-800G 
migration window has compressed to ~3–4 years (2022–25). Our industry research 
suggests the initial 1.6T ramp is likely to begin in 2H26, implying the 800G-to-1.6T 
transition could compress to less than two years.

A s
hortening upgrade cadence is supportive of data center switch ASP expansion. We 
see Accton as a key beneficiary, given its strong positioning in high-speed white-box 
data center switches.

Continued ASP expansion in datacenter switches on content growth and a shorter 
upgrade cadence 
We estimate an 800G bare-metal switch costs a little more than 2x a 400G switch, 
primarily driven by higher content in switch silicon and upgrades in PCB/CCL materials 
required for higher-speed transmission. Looking ahead, 1.6T platforms are expected to 
adopt Tomahawk 6, with CCL material upgrading to M9—providing a continued 
tailwind to ASP expansion. This underscores meaningful revenue upside for Accton as

18

### Page 19

Albert Hung AC 
(886-2) 2725-9875 
albert.hung@jpmchase.com 
Asia Pacific Equity Research 
25 June 2026

800G/1.6T penetration rises.

Speed migration remains the core driver of data center switch ASP expansion. Each 
generational step-up requires more advanced components—switch ICs, CCL/PCBs, 
power delivery, and thermal systems—pushing BoM costs higher. For example, our 
industry research suggests Tomahawk 5 ASP is ~2–3x that of Tomahawk 4.

At the board level, higher speeds typically require both an upgrade in CCL materials and 
a higher PCB layer count to maintain signal integrity. Our research suggests CCL costs 
step up materially in 800G switches, where materials move to M8 versus M6+ in 400G 
designs. Combined with higher layer counts, this can drive a multi-fold increase in 
PCB-related costs, reinforcing the upward trajectory in system ASPs.

Finally, we see incremental upside from broader adoption of liquid cooling in high-end 
switches as switch silicon and optical components become more power-dense and 
hyperscalers increasingly optimize for data center energy efficiency. CPO adoption 
could also significantly increase the switch BoM and bodes well for ODM margins 
given the high complexity, in our view. Our research also indicates that Nvidia’s 
Spectrum-X switch chip has a higher price point vs. Broadcom's Tomahawk solution.

Given the wide variation in switch radix specifications, we focus on ASP per switch port 
in the discussion below. In a new cycle, switching vendors typically capture a 
meaningful ASP premium in the early ramp, followed by ~5% annual ASP erosion as 
volumes scale and the technology matures. As a result, the pace of speed migration—
and the resulting port shipment mix—are the key swing factors for the blended ASP 
trajectory.

For example, while we saw a low-single-digit annual decline in ASP for each individual 
data center switch port speed over 2018–25, the mix shift toward higher-speed ports still 
drove blended ASP growth of ~4% CAGR. In contrast, Gartner® data show that the 
blended ASP for campus switch ports declined by ~1% per year over the same period. 
We attribute this gap to slower speed migration on campus switches, which limits mix-
driven ASP uplift.

Lion's share in Amazon and Meta’s 100G cycle 
Accton has long been a bellwether in the white-box switching ecosystem. In 2015, HP 
collaborated with Accton to develop “BriteBox” switches targeted at 10G/40G 
configurations for CSP customers. In parallel, as a key contributor to Meta’s Open 
Compute Project (OCP) switching efforts, Accton’s wholly owned subsidiary, Edgecore, 
launched the industry’s first open network switch (Wedge-16X) based on Meta’s Wedge 
design and running Facebook Open Switch System (FBOSS). Shipments of Accton’s 
Wedge and BriteBox platforms began in 1H15.

Supported by first-mover advantage, sustained R&D investment, and deep hyperscaler 
relationships, Accton established a dominant position in the white-box data center 
market (50%+ share) during the 100G cycle (2015–21), including an estimated lion’s 
share (80%+) of Amazon and Meta’s 100G switch volumes. In contrast, Google 
primarily outsources switch manufacturing to EMS vendors (CLS); Accton has 
historically opted not to pursue Google’s switch supply chain given less attractive 
margin economics.

M/s shift in Meta’s 200G/400G cycle 
Meta introduced Minipack in March 2019, a 32-port 400G fabric switch built by 
Edgecore Networks. It was the industry’s first 400G open network switch. Minipack

22

### Page 23

Albert Hung AC 
(886-2) 2725-9875 
albert.hung@jpmchase.com 
Asia Pacific Equity Research 
25 June 2026

delivers materially better efficiency versus its predecessor, Backpack, using roughly half 
the power and space, and runs on Meta’s FBOSS software.

That said, 400G did not move into a true volume cycle until 2021–22, when Meta 
launched Minipack 2 (e.g., Wedge 400/400C) and selected Celestica as an ODM partner. 
We attribute the slower-than-expected 400G ramp to limited availability of high-
volume, low-cost 400G optics, early technical friction, and COVID-related disruptions. 
As the 400G program scaled from 2022, these factors contributed to a market share shift 
within Meta from Accton to Celestica.

Clawing back market share in Meta’s 800G cycle in 2026 
Entering the 800G cycle, we expect Accton to retain its leadership position in Amazon’s 
800G deployments while also regaining share within Meta’s 800G program. At OCP 
2024, Meta introduced Minipack 3 / Cisco 8501 800G switches, based on Broadcom 
Tomahawk 5 and Cisco Silicon One G200, and manufactured by Celestica and Wistron, 
respectively.

Meta subsequently announced another OCP 800G platform, Minipack 3N, in October 
2025. The design is based on Nvidia’s Spectrum-X Ethernet switch IC and is positioned 
as a key building block for Meta’s next-generation AI data center infrastructure. Meta 
has selected Accton as the manufacturing partner for this program. Our industry 
research suggests Minipack 3N began an initial ramp in 4Q25, with demand 
strengthening this year—supportive of Accton’s switch revenue momentum.

Potential content upside from CPO switch adoption at the ToR 
layer 
Accton/Edgecore announced a scale-out CPO switch design based on Broadcom’s 
Tomahawk 6 last year. We are seeing rising traction for CPO in AI networking as 
customers push for higher bandwidth density, lower latency, and better power efficiency. 
Nvidia is currently the market leader in CPO switching, but we expect Broadcom to 
narrow the gap as Tomahawk 6 CPO configurations ramp.

For switch ODMs such as Accton, CPO should translate into higher MVA per system 
given greater platform complexity and a higher likelihood of liquid cooling adoption. 
More importantly, CPO architectures integrate optical capabilities (optical engines) 
directly into the switch platform, versus traditional electrical switches that rely on 
pluggable optical transceivers. This shifts a meaningful amount of optical content into 
the switch itself—often a higher-value portion of the bill of materials than the base 
switch system—creating material ASP upside. CPO platforms typically incorporate 
optical engines, fiber alignment units, external laser sources, and associated fiber/cable/ 
connectors.

Overall, we view rising CPO adoption as a positive for Accton, supported by its design 
execution track record, vertical integration capability, and, in our view, improving 
margin mix.

II. Sustained growth momentum of AWS 
ASIC business 
We expect Accton’s AWS ASIC-related revenue to continue growing over the next few 
years, as robust AWS AI demand and incremental content wins more than offset 
headwinds from (1) an m/s shift in the OAM/UBB business and (2) board design 
changes in the Trainium 3 generation.

24

We continue to see solid AWS AI ASIC demand, and view Trainium as a key enabler 
within Amazon’s AI stack. We forecast a ~20% CAGR in Trainium ASIC shipments 
over 2026–28. In addition, our industry research suggests Accton has secured the scale-
up switch tray assembly scope within the Trainium 3 program. We also see incremental 
content opportunities in Trainium 3 boards, including PCIe switches and potential cold 
plates.

Overall, we forecast 17%/30% revenue growth for Accton’s Network Application 
segment in 2026/27. A key downside risk is further delay to the Trn3 project.

Resumed AWS ASIC momentum in 2Q26; demand signals 
turning more constructive 
Accton’s Network Application Equipment (NAE) revenue is now ~10x larger than in 
2017, and the segment’s revenue exposure has expanded to ~62% in 2025 from 12% in 
2017. We attribute this step-change primarily to strong AWS ASIC demand and 
Accton’s leading position in the OAM/UBB ODM business.

AWS ASIC-related revenue momentum has been muted over the past two quarters, 
largely reflecting project transitions. Looking ahead, we expect momentum to re-
accelerate from late 2Q26 as AWS T3 ramp. Importantly, we have seen incremental 
improvement in recent demand indicators, which reinforces our view that AWS AI 
ASIC demand remains on an upward trajectory into 2026/27.

In Amazon’s recent annual shareholder letter, the CEO highlighted an ambition to scale 
the revenue run-rate of its in-house silicon portfolio (including Trainium, Graviton, and 
Nitro) to US$50bn from ~US$20bn, supported in part by monetizing external sales 
opportunities—similar to Google’s approach with TPU. We view this as a constructive 
signal for the broader AWS AI ASIC supply chain, with Accton positioned as a potential 
beneficiary.

OpenAI announced an 8-year compute procurement deal to purchase ~2GW of 
Trainium capacity (including both T3 and T4 skus) through AWS infrastructure, to 
support its general and advanced workloads (link). Additionally, following the Project 
Rainier with ~1mn Trainium 2 procurement by the end of last year, Anthropic signed a 
new agreement with Amazon to secure up to 5GW of new compute, including a total of 
1GW Trainium 2 and Trainium 3 capacity by the end of this year (link). The 10-year 
procurement plan also includes Graviton and Trainium 4 chips, and subsequent-
generation chips.

Based on our sensitivity analysis below, each GW procurement deal for AWS’s 
Trainium chips (T3 in our exercise) represents ~US$2.1bn of incremental revenue TAM 
for Accton, comprising ~US$1.4bn for chip modules and ~US$700mn for switch trays. 
Assuming ~80%/~50% m/s for Accton in ASIC modules and NRL (NeuronLink) switch 
trays, respectively, we estimate ~US$1.4bn of incremental revenue for Accton—
equivalent to 18% of Accton’s total revenue in 2025.

Potential m/s shift in Trn3; scale-up switch tray wins could 
largely offset the downside 
Our industry research suggests AWS is onboarding additional ODM suppliers (e.g., 
Fabrinet, Wiwynn, Flextronics) for Trn3 OAM/UBB programs, which could drive 
market share dilution versus prior generations. That said, we expect Accton to retain its 
leadership position, maintaining 60%+ m/s over the next 2-3 years. In our view, the 
incremental scope from the Trn3 scale-up switch tray program could offset much of the 
potential downside from any share shift in OAM/UBB.

Lower board content per chip, partially offset by PCB material 
upgrades and potential new content gains 
Trainium 3’s liquid-cooled compute board architecture moves to four ASICs per board 
versus two ASICs per board in Trainium 2. While this mechanically lowers board 
content per ASIC, we estimate that liquid cooling adoption will remain limited at ~20% 
for the Trainium 3 generation this year.

We also see offsetting content upgrades in UBB boards, led by copper foil material 
migration (HVLP 2 to HVLP 4) and potential incremental content wins (e.g., PCIe 
switch). Overall, we estimate broadly stable OAM+UBB content per chip for Accton at 
~US$2,000 this year, but model a gradual decline in 2027/2028 as liquid cooling 
penetration increases.

III. Growing TAM opportunity through vertical 
integration 
Marching toward the server space 
Accton is increasingly positioning itself as a rack-level AI infrastructure partner, aiming 
to offer an end-to-end solution spanning both switching and server domains. Its 
portfolio extends from switch systems, NICs, optical transceivers, and cables to 
accelerator modules—and ultimately server systems.

From a content perspective, networking typically represents less than 10% of CSP cloud 
capex, while server spend can account for ~50–60%, implying meaningful expansion 
runway if Accton can scale beyond switching into servers. Management expects an 
initial contribution from the server business in 2H26, starting with smaller-scale 
customers.

Figure 42: Accton’s vision of vertical integration in datacenter networking and compute

Source: Accton’s presentation.

Gauging the potential server revenue upside 
Although Accton’s server business remains nascent, we believe the company can 
leverage its track record in switch system design and manufacturing. In the section 
below, we frame the potential upside should Accton achieve a meaningful server-side 
breakthrough.

We expect Accton to begin with white-box, general-purpose servers for smaller-scale 
customers to build a track record and establish manufacturing credibility. Accordingly, 
we exclude high-end AI server platforms (e.g., NVL72, HGX, AI ASIC servers) from 
the TAM framework below.

29

### Page 30

Albert Hung AC 
(886-2) 2725-9875

25 June 2026

Assuming a ~US$12k ASP for general-purpose servers and that white-box U.S. CSP 
servers represent ~50% of total general-purpose server shipments, we estimate an 
addressable server revenue TAM of ~US$74bn for Accton. Given these assumptions, 
each 1% market share gain in the U.S. CSP general-purpose server market could 
translate into ~9% upside to Accton’s 2025 revenue. Importantly, there is additional 
upside if Accton can ultimately expand into the AI server market.

Promising opportunities in TAM expansion 
Beyond servers, we see meaningful TAM expansion potential for Accton across both 
components and switch systems.

Component level: upside from optics and other critical rack-level components 
Accton’s wholly owned subsidiary, Edgecore, offers optical transceivers and cable 
products that customers can select alongside its switching solutions. Accton has also 
established Goldilink to develop 800G optical solutions to support next-generation high- 
speed data transmission. Beyond optics, Accton is positioning itself as a broader system 
integrator, with ambitions to supply other critical non-optical rack-level components, 
such as liquid cooling. For instance, Accton designs its own L2A/L2L liquid-cooling 
systems for its high-end datacenter switches.

technology is a high-resolution optical 
device that is used in Wavelength

specific light wavelengths by applying 
voltage patterns to liquid crystal cells and 
enabling dynamic, flexible bandwidth 
control without moving parts. Key 
advantages of LCOS switches over

Systems) include scalability (high radix), 
low latency, and power efficiency. 
Source: Company data.

Networking switch system level: expansion into scale-up and scale-across switches

Today, Accton’s networking business is primarily focused on scale-out switching (i.e., 
ToR switches). That said, the company has been engaging with a key U.S. CSP 
customer on a customized scale-up switch (switch tray) for ASIC training clusters, and 
we expect an initial revenue contribution beginning in 2H26.

Looking further ahead, Accton is also targeting scale-across/DCI switching, which 
enables interconnection between data center clusters. The company is focusing on 
LCoS-based WSS (Wavelength Selective Switch) and appears well positioned in this 
technology. Accton announced a US$29mn investment in InLC Technology (private) for 
LCoS capability in November last year.

Key downside risks 
Potential m/s shift in Ethernet switch: competition with Nvidia 
Our research indicates that customers have shifted to Ethernet-based switches from 
Infiniband switches over the past 3 years, leading to Infiniband adoption declining to 
below one-third of the AI datacenter switch market this year from a dominant solution 
(~80%) 3 years ago. We attribute this to a step-up in Ethernet switch configuration to 
capture the AI networking switch TAM. Nvidia also rolled out its first high-performance 
Ethernet switch product line - Spectrum switch (SN5000 series) in 2023, followed by 
subsequent line-ups (i.e. Spectrum-X Photonics, SN6000 series).

As Neoclouds typically lack in-house engineering resources, they tend to follow 
Nvidia’s reference designs when building GPU server networking clusters. As a result, 
Nvidia has been gaining share in the data center Ethernet switch market over the past 
two years, supported by fast-growing neocloud AI demand. However, hyperscalers are 
used to adopting in-house networking architectures for AI clusters and outsourcing bare-
metal Ethernet switches to white-box ODM vendors such as Celestica and Accton.

While we see CSPs (e.g., Meta) adopting Nvidia’s Spectrum switch silicon in certain 
deployments versus Broadcom alternatives, CSPs still co-develop their in-house system 
designs with white-box switch ODM partners. We therefore expect bare-metal switches

32

### Page 33

Albert Hung AC 
(886-2) 2725-9875 
albert.hung@jpmchase.com 
Asia Pacific Equity Research 
25 June 2026

to remain the dominant form factor in CSP data centers at the ToR layer, given their 
meaningful cost advantage. We also see potential opportunity for whitebox Ethernet 
scale-up AI datacenter switch adoption in CSPs’ ASIC clusters, which could be an 
incremental market for Accton.

Notably, the industry economics also reinforce this structure: branded Ethernet switch 
vendors (e.g., Arista, Cisco, Juniper, Nvidia) typically generate 50%+ GMs, versus 
~15–25% GMs for switch ODMs.

Faster-than-expected m/s shift in AWS ASIC module biz 
As Amazon’s ASIC module program scales, we are seeing new entrants increasingly 
penetrate the AWS AI ASIC module supply chain, including Flextronics, Wiwynn, and 
Fabrinet. Fabrinet has already begun T2 ASIC module production in 2H25, while our 
industry research suggests Wiwynn could enter via the T3 program (MP in 2H26). 
Flextronics also appears to be a credible potential entrant.

Despite this share dilution risk, we expect Accton to retain its leadership position and 
NPI role (60%+) given its long-standing partnership with Amazon. Moreover, we 
believe ongoing content expansion—driven by PCB/CCL material upgrades, higher 
layer counts, and rising liquid-cooling adoption—along with incremental project scope 
wins (e.g., NRL switch trays) can more than offset the headwind from a lower module 
share. Overall, we continue to expect Accton’s NAE segment to deliver sustained 
revenue growth, with Amazon remaining the largest contributor.

Company Background 
Company history 
Founded in Taiwan Hsinchu Science Park in 1988, Accton started as an entry-level 
Ethernet card/hub ODM/OEM vendor and has become a world-leading networking and 
communication supplier for global top-tier customers. Accton stock was listed on the 
TWSE and has been trading since 15 Nov 1995.

In 2001, the company announced a merger with Hexiang Communication and entered 
the WLAN market. In 2003, Accton separated its Wireless Network Business Division 
to establish Arcadyan Tech Corp., a joint venture with Philips. Later, Accton purchased 
all the shares of the subsidiary from Philips and partially transferred them to Compal in 
2006.

In 2007, Accton established a joint venture with Alvarion, the leading supplier of 
WiMAX in Israel, and acquired the 802.16e mobile WiMAX bid from the Industrial 
Technology Research Institute (ITRI), equipping itself to provide complete WiMAX and 
Wi-Fi integration solutions.

In 2010, Accton and LG-Nortel established a joint venture, Edgecore Networks, in the 
U.S., focusing on delivering branded wired and wireless networking products and 
solutions for data center, service provider, enterprise, and SMB customers worldwide.

Accton joined Meta’s OCP (Open Compute Project) alliance in 2012 and has been 
acting as one of the key contributors to the organization. Accton already delivered a 10G 
ToR switch design to OCP in 2014 as the first certified networking equipment design. 
The company launched the Wedge -16X running on Facebook Operating System 
(FBOSS) in 2015.

Business overview 
In 2025, Accton derived 35% of its revenue from the Switch business and 62% from the 
Network Application segment. Other segments include Metro Access (1%), Wireless 
(0%), and Others (2%). Accton's products are mainly sold to the United States (81%), 
Europe (7%), Asia (4%), and Taiwan (7%). 
• Network Application segment includes mainly AWS Networking interface cards, 
ASIC chip module assembly, and potential AWS’s ASIC scale-up switch trays. 
Segment revenue grew 290% YoY in 2025 and became the largest business, driven 
by strong AWS ASIC demand. 
• Switch business includes the datacenter and enterprise switch businesses, with the 
datacenter whitebox switch business comprising the majority (60%+). Amazon and 
Meta are key datacenter switch customers, while HPE, Juniper, and Huawei are key 
enterprise switch customers. We have seen accelerating revenue growth for the 
business in 2025 driven by high-end datacenter switch upgrades (400G/800G). 
• Metro Access business is mainly carrier/telco related applciations. Key products 
include edge switches and routers. 
• Wireless products include fixed wireless access (FWA), CPE, and Wi-Fi access 
point equipment.

Competitive analysis 
Accton and Celestica are the two leading players in datacenter whitebox ToR switching, 
followed by Quanta and other smaller players. We attribute the higher consolidation in 
the datacenter whitebox switch market, relative to the server whitebox market, to the 
more complex switch configurations and thus higher requirements for design as well as 
software capabilities. We believe Accton’s competitive edge has helped the company 
secure its leading position in whitebox datacenter switches for the past decade and will 
likely continue this trend in the future. 
• Early mover in whitebox datacenter switching 
Accton is a key contributor to Meta’s OCP switch and launched the industry's first OCP-
approved 10G/40G/100G switches. Accton also partnered with Meta to design and 
manufacture Wedge 40G/100G datacenter switches. While Celestica has become a key 
partner for Meta’s Wedge 200G/400G and Minipack 3 (800G), Meta also announced the 
Minipack 3N during the 2025 OCP and assigned Accton as the manufacturing partner. 
We believe Accton’s continued R&D investment could help it maintain its leading 
position in future cycles.

• Accelerating R&D investment in advanced switch development 
Accton’s R&D intensity peaked at ~6% in 2014 before its penetration into datacenter 
whitebox switching. While R&D spending continued to increase during 2015-2019, 
R&D intensity declined due to faster-growing revenues. We began to see accelerating 
growth in both R&D dollars and intensity from 2020 onward, driven by strong high-
speed networking demand. We attribute this to the COVID-driven acceleration in 
digitalization trends and the rise of AIGC. Looking ahead, we anticipate growing R&D 
spending by Accton in the next few years, driven by the secular high-end switch 
upgrade cycle and likely increasing product offerings in AI datacenters.

Compared to Celestica, Accton has much higher R&D spending amount and intensity. 
Accton’s R&D intensity was 4.3-4.5% in 2023/24 and decreased to 2.8% in 2025 due to 
strong top-line growth momentum (+120% YoY), while Celestica’s R&D intensity was 
0.8%/1% in the past two years. Despite the narrowing gap of R&D intensity, Accton’s 
R&D spending was ~US$224mn in 2025, almost doubling that of Celestica.

• Vertical integration with innovative solutions 
Accton is capable of providing total solutions by supplying networking subsystems and 
systems at both server-to-switch (e.g., networking adapters, AI chip modules) and 
switch-to-switch ends. The company is also able to supply AI server systems, optical 
transceivers, and cables (both DAC and AOC).

Accton is also developing innovative technologies in accelerated networking such as 
liquid-cooled switches, LPO (Linear-drive Pluggable Optics), and CXL (Compute 
Express Link) switches to help customers reduce power consumption, enhance overall 
AI system performance, and optimize total cost of ownership.

For example, Accton introduced the industry’s first two-phase immersion-cooled 
100G/400G switches in 2022 and shipped POC (proof of concept) samples to customers 
for qualification and co-development. Although the new technology is still in its nascent 
stages, we believe Accton will differentiate itself from peers with potential ASP/margin 
premium when the new technology matures. 
• Strong partnerships with upstream chip vendors and software developers 
Accton is a strategic partner with leading switch silicon vendors (e.g., Broadcom) as 
well as CPU vendors (e.g., Intel). Switch JDM partners such as Accton need to 
participate in the early development of a switch vendor’s next-generation chip and work 
closely with chip vendors on the entire system design. For example, the increased power 
consumption and port count of next-generation switch chips may necessitate design 
changes in thermal management, power supply, optical components, and mechanical 
parts. Higher-speed transmissions also require PCB material upgrades and potential 
layout modifications.

In addition, Accton is committed to developing an open network architecture to provide 
customers with products that are highly flexible and offer better TCO. Network 
operating systems play a crucial role in both enterprise and datacenter networks by 
ensuring proper resource allocation (e.g., storage, memory, network bandwidth), 
security, troubleshooting, and scaling.

Open-source NOS has become increasingly important and widely adopted in datacenter 
switches due to its flexibility, cost-effectiveness, and scalability. Accton, as an early 
contributor to the OCP Summit, supports most of the key open NOS, including Open 
Network Linux (ONL), PicOS (developed by Pica8), SONiC (Software for Open 
Networking in the Cloud, developed by Microsoft), and Cumulus Linux (developed by 
Cumulus Networks), just to name a few. Accton’s strong footprint in open network 
systems could help the company secure its leadership in the networking switch space, in 
our view.

Diversified Production Sites 
Accton has diversified production sites in Taiwan, Vietnam, the U.S., and China. Accton 
is also expanding capacity in Taiwan, Vietnam, the U.S., Singapore, and Malaysia to 
support strong customer demand. Additionally, Accton sees a talent shortage in Taiwan 
and is looking for new talent overseas to support future projects.

investor exceeds 10% of the shareholdings.

Income statement

switch project, and new content wins in the AWS ASIC project. We forecast a ~50% 
earnings CAGR for Accton through 2025-2028, driven by strong top-line sales growth.

Glossary 
Switch (or Network Switch) 
A switch is a networking hardware device used to connect servers within a datacenter or 
a fixed area. A switch serves as a controller, allowing networked devices to 
communicate with one another effectively, which can enhance overall information 
sharing and operational efficiency.

Figure 71: Network switch

“Switches create a network. Routers 
connect networks. The two pieces of 
equipment look similar and perform some 
similar functions, but each has its own 
distinct function to perform on a network.” 
by Cisco 
Source: Company data.

Router 
While switches allow different devices on a network to communicate, routers allow 
different networks to communicate, forming an even larger network. A router can also 
connect networked devices to the Internet, allowing multiple users to share a 
connection. Technically, a router works as a dispatcher, directing data traffic and 
choosing the best route for information to travel across the network, so that it is 
transmitted as efficiently as possible.

Figure 72: Router

Source: Company data.

44

### Page 45

Albert Hung AC 
(886-2) 2725-9875 
albert.hung@jpmchase.com 
Asia Pacific Equity Research 
25 June 2026

Top-of-Rack (ToR) 
In Top-of-Rack (ToR) switching architecture, all the servers located within one rack are 
connected to an in-rack network switch (ToR switch), which is, in turn, connected to 
aggregation switches via fiber optic cables. Despite its name, the actual physical 
location of the ToR switch does not necessarily need to be at the top of the rack. 
However, the top-of-the-rack position is most common due to easier accessibility and 
cleaner cable management.

The key benefit of the ToR design is that all copper cabling for servers stays within the 
rack, allowing each rack to be deployed as an individual and modular unit within the 
data center. This allows the system to be upgraded to higher speeds in the future with 
minimal cost and leveraging the existing cabling. However, the ToR design might also 
require a greater number of switches and higher maintenance due to the distributed 
architecture.

Figure 73: Top of Rack (ToR) structure

Source: FS.com. 
An alternative design to ToR is End-of-Row (EoR) architecture, wherein each server in 
a rack is directly connected to a common aggregation switch. The EoR design requires 
fewer switches to manage and, in turn, fewer ports in the aggregation switch; however, 
it requires an expensive, bulky optical interconnect due to the longer distance.

Leaf-Spine structure 
The leaf-spine architecture is a network topology that consists of two switching layers: 
leaf and spine. The leaf layer consists of ToR/EoR switches that aggregate traffic from 
servers and connect directly to the spine or network core. Spine switches are typically 
aggregation switches that interconnect all of the leaf switches in a full-mesh topology. 
The path is randomly chosen so that the traffic load is evenly distributed among the top-
tier switches.

45

### Page 46

Albert Hung AC 
(886-2) 2725-9875 
albert.hung@jpmchase.com 
Asia Pacific Equity Research 
25 June 2026

Figure 74: Traditional 3-tier Architecture vs. Spine-Leaf Architecture

Source: Aruba Networks. 
The leaf-spine architecture provides low and predictable latency since traffic always has 
to cross the same number of switch devices to reach another server. This is critical as 
applications are increasingly deployed in a distributed fashion that relies more on East-
West traffic.

Additionally, this design enables straightforward capacity expansion as an additional 
spine or leaf switch can be added and links can be extended to each spine or leaf switch 
without reconfiguration of the entire network.

JDM/ ODM business models 
The traditional ODM business model does not require software support from customers. 
However, the JDM model features a closer partnership between customers (such as 
hyperscalers) and manufacturing partners (such as Accton), with customers involved in 
the product design flow. Typically, the JDM model has higher customer retention (closer 
collaboration with customers) and a better margin profile (due to higher software 
content) compared to the ODM model.

For instance, under a JDM model for switch products, hyperscalers first choose the 
semi-finished firmware from among 100+ versions of the datacenter OS available 
(based on the Linux architecture), and Accton’s engineering team then completes the 
rest of the firmware work based on customer needs. Afterwards, Accton selects the 
suitable chipsets and hardware components for hyperscalers and finishes the product 
design and manufacturing for customers.

Silicon photonics 
Silicon photonics (SiPh) involves the use of silicon semiconductors made of LD and 
PD using epi wafers as the medium for optical signals, allowing much faster digital 
signaling than traditional electron-based semiconductor devices. Recently, major U.S. 
internet companies such as Facebook and Google have been working to build optical 
data centers, where silicon photonics is expected to be deployed. According to Yole 
Research, the silicon photonics device market is expected to grow from less than US 
$25mn in 2013 to more than US$700mn in 2024 with a 38% CAGR, supported by 
increasing demand from the emerging optical data centers.

Investment Thesis, Valuation and Risks 
Accton (Overweight; Price Target: NT$3,600.00) 
Investment Thesis 
Accton stock trades around the high end of its historical valuation range, reflecting its strong 
earnings outlook, high ROE, and expanding TAM. We expect an accelerating earnings 
CAGR over 2025–28 for Accton, driven by rapid 800G/1.6T switch upgrades, share gains 
in Meta’s ToR, and a resumption of AWS ASIC momentum. TAM expansion into server 
racks and optical adjacency offers further upside optionality. OW. 
Valuation 
Our Jun-27 PT of NT$3,600 is based on 12M fwd EPS, at the higher end of historical up-
cycle valuations, given the accelerating datacenter whitebox switch upgrade cycle, secular 
AI demand, and potential re-rating opportunity from the optical biz. 
Risks to Rating and Price Target 
Key downside risks include potential share loss in AWS AI ASIC modules, slower-than-
expected 800G/1.6T adoption, and intensifying competition from Nvidia and OEMs in 
Ethernet switches.

ABF 載板龍頭廠產能爆滿 
 本業獲利持續向上，疊加業外貢獻，預估 2Q26 EPS 6.39 元：4 月
份自結 EPS 1.85 元。5 月份營收 141 億元 ，+1%MOM，+32%YOY，
+27%累計 YOY，本業向上，5 月份聯電股價上漲 87%，將挹注欣興
業外。推估 2Q26 營收 437 億元，+17%QOQ，+35%YOY，毛利率
提升至 23.6%，營益率 15.2%，單季稅後 EPS 6.39 元。 
 需求強勁，欣興產能爆滿，將持續擴產：AI GPU、ASIC、CPU 三大
需求驅動下 ， 目前欣興 ABF 高階廠區光復廠 、 楊梅廠維持全產運作，
Legacy 廠區稼動率亦達 90%以上高檔水準，整體產能供給相當吃
緊。擴產方面，客戶提前支付款項以參與擴產 ，光復一廠三期預計
2H26 開出，推估 2026 年底產能相較去年底增加 40%，後續光復二
廠及楊梅二廠則分別規劃於 2H27 及 2028 年投產，高階廠區各廠皆
可再極大化產能，可額外增加 15-18%產能。 
 維持買進評等：預估 2026 年營收 1888 億元，+44%YOY，毛利率
23.8%，營益率 14.4%，推估全年稅後 EPS 估 18.55 元。預期 2027
年 ABF 載板供需缺口擴大至三成水準 ， 帶動價格持續上揚 ， 預估2027
年稅後 EPS 為 30.25 元 ， 維持買進 ， 目標價1300 元(43x2027EPS)。

欣興為全球 IC 載板領導廠商

欣興電子為全球印刷電路板與 IC 載板製造領導廠商，屬於聯電旗下，總部位於
桃園龜山；產品涵蓋 ABF 載板、BT 載板、高密度連接板(HDI)、多層板、FPC
及軟硬結合板等，廣泛應用於AI 伺服器、高效能運算、網通設備、智慧型手機、
消費性電子及車用電子等領域 。目前生產基地位於台灣、中國、泰國 等地區，
ABF/BT 載板、HDI 主要在台灣、中國生產，其中高階ABF 廠區位於新竹光復、
桃園楊梅，至於 PCB 主要在泰國進行生產。

2025 年產品營收佔比：Substrate 58%(ABF 46% +BT 12%)、HDI 27%、PCB 
11%、FPC 2%、 其他1%。元富預估 2026 年 Substrate 佔比 63%，+57%YOY，
2026-2027 年成長以 ABF 為主，預期 2026 年 ABF 載板+72%YOY，BT 載板
+0%YOY，HDI 佔比 24%、PCB 佔比 10%、FPC 佔比 2%、其他佔比 1%。

1Q26 產品組合轉佳，單季 稅後
EPS 3.28 元

1Q26 營收 374 億元，+8%QOQ，+25%YOY，1)產品別營收佔比：Substrate 
59%(ABF 48%+BT 11%)、HDI 27%、PCB 11%、FPC 2%、其他 1%，2)應用
別營收佔比：Computers 65%、Communication 22%、Consumer & Others 9%、
Automotive 4%；獲利方面，毛利率 18%，本業獲利 27.6 億元，營益率 7.4%，
符合預期，本業獲利大幅成長，係因 AI GPU、ASIC 客戶對高階 ABF 載板需求
持續推升， 稼動率高檔， 產品組合轉佳； 至於業外持有聯電(2303)、 京元電(2449)
等股票，享有金融資產評價利益(聯電 1Q26 上漲 15%、京元電上漲 3%)，合計
業外收入 35.4 億元；稅前淨利 63 億元，歸屬母公司稅後淨利 50.4 億元，單季
稅後 EPS 3.28 元。

本業獲利持續 向上，疊加業外貢
獻，預估 2Q26 EPS 6.39 元

欣興 4 月份營收 139 億元，+7%MOM，+28%YOY，+25%累計 YOY，稅前淨
利 34.6 億元，4 月份本業獲利略高於業外收入，顯示本業持續向上，歸屬母公
司淨利 28.44 億元，單月自結 EPS 1.85 元。

5 月份營收 141 億元，+1%MOM，+32%YOY，+27%累計 YOY，5 月份聯電股
價上漲 87%，將挹注欣興業外金融資產評價利益，預期 5 月份本業、業外獲利
皆較 4 月份優異。元富推估 2Q26 營收 437 億元，+17%QOQ，+35%YOY，毛
利率提升至 23.6%，營益率 15.2%，單季稅後 EPS 6.39 元。

需求強勁， 欣興產能爆滿 ，將持
續擴產

展望後市，欣興主要成長動能來自於 ABF 載板，AI HDI 與光模組(mSAP)等 AI
相關產品展望亦佳；預估 2026 年載板業務(ABF+BT)營收佔比可望回到 2022
年高峰水準 60%以上。ABF 載板需求方面，AI GPU 、ASIC 及 CPU 三大需求
同步增溫，1)Rubin 世代 GPU 載板目前仍以 Ibiden(日)與欣興為主要供應商，
2)ASIC：欣興歷經與客戶多年產品共同開發後，已成多數 ASIC 客戶主要載板
供應商，3)受 Agentic AI 帶動 CPU 需求影響，2Q26 以來 CPU 訂單亦明顯回升 ，
進一步推升 ABF 需求。

產能方面，目前高階廠區光復廠、楊梅廠維持全產運作，Legacy 廠區稼動率亦
達 90%以上高檔水準，整體產能供給相當吃緊，現階段不論高階廠區或 Legacy
廠區均呈現爆單狀態，新產能開出後迅速被客戶需求消化；至於 BT 方面，公司
持續推動 BT 產線轉供 ABF， 高階BT 產品維持生產 ， 中低階BT 產能逐步調整，
2026 年 BT 產能預計縮減 15%，以支援 ABF 擴產需求。

擴產計畫方面，光復一廠三期預計 2H26 開出，推估 2026 年底產能相較去年底
增加 40%，後續光復二廠及楊梅二廠則分別規劃於 2H27 及 2028 年投產，高階
廠區每廠最有效率產能為 6000-7000 片/月(20inch*20inch)，各廠皆可再極大化
產能，可額外增加 15-18%產能。目前客戶提前支付金額已超過光復二及楊梅二
兩廠資本支出總額，顯示客戶 積極尋求產能並對未來產能需求具高度信心 ；楊
梅廠區仍保有進一步 擴充楊梅三、楊梅四之空間，未來將視需求與客戶承諾情
況決定擴產進度。 
Exhibit 10: 欣興高階廠區擴產計畫

Sources: Masterlink

HDI 產品方面，AI HDI 及光模組(mSAP)亦為欣興另一重要成長引擎；蘆竹廠
AI HDI 設備 1Q26 完成裝機，2Q26 開始出貨，預期將對毛利率帶來正面貢獻；
1) Compute Board 佔 HDI 營收 50%，2)至於光模組，欣興為全球主要 mSAP
供應商 ， 受惠800G 及 1.6T 光通訊需求快速成長 ，2026 年光模組訂單量較去年
成長 2-3 倍，佔 HDI 營收比重已自 2025 年的 10-15%提升至目前的 15-20%，
公司預計 2H26 再增加 40-50%產能(山鶯廠)，但即便擴產，整體市場仍呈供不
應求狀態。

維持買進評等

整體而言，預估 2026 年 AI 相關產品營收佔比 60%，若將光模組納入計算，則
佔比將進一步提高；隨產品組合持續優化、高階 ABF 佔比提升，以及 AI HDI、
光模組放量，推估未來高階產品毛利率有望逐步朝 40-50%區間邁進。元富預估
2026 年營收 1888 億元，+44%YOY，毛利率 23.8%，營益率 14.4%，推估全
年稅後 EPS 估 18.55 元。由圖六預估，預期2027 年 ABF 載板供需缺口擴大至
三成水準，帶動價格持續上揚，預估 2027 年欣興稅後 EPS 為 30.25 元，維持
買進評等，目標價 1300 元(43x2027EPS)。

 水冷零組件訂單更趨明確

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
公司成立於 1991 年， 主要從事鋁合金材料之製造及銷售 ，2017 年敏實集團入主後轉
型發展汽車零件事業。當前終端銷售地區以中國大陸及歐洲為主，佔比分別為 77%、
23%。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 110 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，96 
【大盤指數/股價】46465 / 85.5

➢ 1Q26 毛利率受鋁價提升所影響 估後續影響有所緩和 
回顧 1Q26 財報，營收為 19.56 億元，優於預期的 18.89 億元。毛利率
則為 10.4%，低於預期的 14.3%，低於預期主因為受到原物料鋁價上漲
所影響，1Q26 LME 鋁價自 3,100 元左右上漲至 3,450 元左右，帶動原
物料成本大幅上揚，使公司毛利率成壓，公司已向客戶轉嫁成本，預估
鋁價的影響將在 2Q26 有所緩和，因此預期 2Q26 毛利率將較 1Q26 有
所回溫。1Q26 營收 19.56 億元，QoQ-7.27%，YoY+6.38%。EPS 0.02
元，QoQ-94.07%，YoY-94.58%。 
➢ 2H26 水冷相關產品持續送樣 私募案鞏固與客戶上下游合作關係 
展望後市，精確的非車用產品線拓展積極，公司於近期公告董事會決議
辦理私募現增案，將引進策略投資人，總發行股數在 7,000 萬股以內，
此私募案主要為 AI 伺服器散熱領域之客戶 ， 目的為鞏固上下游產業鏈關
係，預期引入該投資人後能建立長期且穩定的供應鏈合作關係。目前散
熱相關產品包括分歧管、浸沒式機櫃、CDU 及 BBU 等產品，皆進行密
集的送樣認證中，送樣品項多元，估計對於下半年出貨有一定產品之能
見度 。 研究部預估2026 年水冷相關零組件營收占比貢獻約落在 5%~7%
左右，在 2027 年期望透過與客戶之策略合作關係，取得更多水冷相關
零組件訂單，預計營收貢獻約落在 12%上下，帶動產品組合持續轉佳。
研究員預估 2Q26 營收 22.61 億元 ，QoQ+15.59%，YoY+24.98%。EPS 
0.38 元，QoQ+1,541.14%，YoY-14.27%。 
➢ 電動車滲透率仍在提升且 2026 年將有水冷業務貢獻營收 維持買
進投資建議 
水冷零組件在策略投資人加入後能有更明確的產能規劃，預估在
2026~2027 年分別有 5% / 12%的營收佔比，帶動產品組合轉佳。人型
機器人則帶來潛在的想像成長空間。研究員預估 2026 年/2027 年營收
分別為 105.18 億元/135.93 億元，YoY+36.61% / 29.23%。EPS 2.74
元/5.74 元，YoY+86.54% / 196.64%。考量電動車市場仍在擴張，且有
潛在新業務將於 2026 年開始貢獻營收 ，維持買進投資建議 ， 目標價110
元(2027F PE 19x)。

個股報告

公司簡介 
公司成立於 1991 年 ， 主要從事鋁合金材料之製造及銷售 ，2017 年敏實集團入主後轉
型發展汽車零件事業。當前終端銷售地區以中國大陸及歐洲為主，佔比分別為 77%、
23%。而生產基地包括 8 座工廠，包括台灣桃園、中國的咸寧、廣州、湖州、嘉興、
東台及歐洲的賽爾維亞。當前之主要產品線包括電池盒、壓鑄產品、鋁型材 +天窗、
保護膜+生產線等四個產品線。 
電動車之電池盒主要功能為保護、支撐電池組，透過高強度材料如鋁合金、鋼鐵和複
合材料包圍電池模組，抵禦外部衝擊。此外還有防止水分、灰塵侵入及防火防爆等功
能，為電動車不可或缺的零組件。 
而壓鑄產品線之產品則包括電控箱、電機殼、電池隔板等。主要位於歐洲進行生產，
因其地理位置優越，靠近歐洲汽車中心，且當地政府提供設備投資稅額抵免及 10 年
企業所得稅免稅等投資優惠，使精確前往塞爾維亞設廠。 
圖 1：產品營收佔比                            單位：%

資料來源：國票投顧 
產業概況 
公司當前之電池盒產品主要應用於中國汽車市場之電池盒產品，因此與中國車市息息
相關。根據過往資料顯示，中國為全球最主要之汽車市場，若以銷量作為區分，中國
約佔全球銷售份額的 34%，為全球最大。其次則為美國，約佔全球銷量的18.8%。再
來則是印度、日本及德國，佔比分別為6.1%、5.2%及 3.4%。而中國汽車之年銷售量
呈現穩定增長之趨勢，2024 年銷售量來到 3,143 萬輛左右，2014 年銷售量則約為
2,349 萬輛 ， 過去10 年 CAGR 約為 2.9%。2025/01~06 中國汽車出貨量則來到 1,565
萬輛，YoY+11.4%，預估 2026 年整體車市將呈現穩定增長之態勢。 
在電動車方面 ， 中國之電動車銷量佔比也逐年提升 。 根據資料顯示 ，2021 年中國電動
車之銷售佔比僅約 13%~15%，在 2024 年提升到 40%左右，預期 2025 年電動車銷
售佔比有望提升至 50%以上 ， 並在2030 年達到 80%的滲透率 ， 為電池盒帶來市場產
值的擴張，對精確而言是正向的產業發展趨勢。 
1Q26 毛利率受鋁價提升所影響 估後續影響有所緩和 
回顧 1Q26 財報，營收為 19.56 億元，優於預期的 18.89 億元。毛利率則為 10.4%，
低於預期的 14.3%，低於預期主因為受到原物料鋁價上漲所影響，1Q26 LME 鋁價自
3,100 元左右上漲至 3,450 元左右，帶動原物料成本大幅上揚，使公司毛利率成壓，
公司已向客戶轉嫁成本，預估鋁價的影響將在 2Q26 有所緩和，因此預期 2Q26 毛利
率將較 1Q26 有所回溫。1Q26 營收 19.56 億元，QoQ-7.27%，YoY+6.38%。EPS 
0.02，QoQ-94.07%，YoY-94.58%。

個股報告 
2H26 水冷相關產品持續送樣 私募案鞏固與客戶上下游合作關係  
展望後市，精確的非車用產品線拓展積極，公司於近期公告董事會決議辦理私募現增
案，將引進策略投資人，總發行股數在7,000 萬股以內，此私募案主要為AI 伺服器散
熱領域之客戶，目的為鞏固上下游產業鏈關係，預期引入該投資人後能建立長期且穩
定的供應鏈合作關係。在散熱零組件方面，水冷板及分岐管等水冷零組件持續進行送
樣中，合作對象包括台系上市即未上市之散熱領導廠商，在散熱零組件供不應求狀況
下使散熱廠有外包產能之需求，使精確這類的車用跨入散熱領域廠商受惠。目前散熱
相關產品包括分歧管、浸沒式機櫃、CDU 及 BBU 等產品， 皆進行密集的送樣認證中 ，
送樣品項多元，估計對於下半年出貨有一定產品之能見度。而水冷零組件毛利率約在
30%以上，也將大幅改善產品組合。研究部預估 2026 年水冷相關零組件營收占比貢
獻約落在 5%~7%左右，在 2027 年期望透過與客戶之策略合作關係，取得更多水冷
相關零組件訂單，預計營收貢獻約落在 12%上下，帶動產品組合持續轉佳。研究員預
估 2Q26 營收 22.61 億 元 ， QoQ+15.59% ， YoY+24.98% 。 EPS 0. 38 元 ，
QoQ+1,541.14%，YoY-14.27%。 
2026 年電池盒營收穩定成長 水冷零組件提升獲利 
展望後市 ，目前電池盒產品線訂單能見度可看到3~5 年 ， 主因汽車產業之特性為產品
週期較長 ， 一但打入後就不會輕易的更換供應商 ， 使目前訂單能見度最遠可達到2030
年。湖州廠在 2026 年將有奇瑞、長城等新客戶加入，廣州廠在 2H25~2026 年將有
北汽極狐、日產等客戶加入，湖北廠則有長安、小鵬及商用車平台之需求，三廠合計
在手訂單總額提升至 60 億人民幣以上，對照當前公司 2025 年營收規模 77 億元左右
之水準，可見在手訂單總額高，未來營運仍有望持續穩定成長。在壓鑄產品線方面主
要生產電池隔板。自2021 年開始接到福斯及 Stellantis 集團訂單，並在2023 年下半
年開始為 Stellantis 及福斯的 MEB 兩大供應商量產。2025 剛開始生產 VOLVO 及
MOBIS 訂單，預估 2025 年可貢獻 4 億人民幣之營收，展望未來，歐洲壓鑄產品線之
在手訂單能見度同樣長達 3~5 年以上，訂單總額則達到 21 億人民幣之規模，訂單仍
有延續性。 
非車用方面布局除了上述提及的水冷零組件外，公司也積極在人型機器人方面進行拓
展，公司已與盟立集團合作，成立奧威科技，為台灣少有的自主品牌機器人廠商。精
確將扮演機器人外構件、骨骼件的壓鑄件廠商，而盟立則負責關鍵關節如諧波減速器
的提供廠商，並透過與母公司敏實集團的合作來找尋產品出海口，期望透過各公司不
同能力的專長發揮來進攻人型機器人市場 ， 雖短期1~2 年內營收貢獻預計不大 ，但人
型機器人為未來數十年重要之產業，將為公司帶來較大的成長想像空間。  
電動車滲透率仍在提升且 2026 年將有水冷業務貢獻營收 維持買進投資建議 
展望 2026~2027 年，目前中國電動車滲透率仍在持續提升中，精確透過過去幾年的
學習經驗，已在電動車電池盒之生產及成本管理達到一定程度之經濟規模，使客戶的
廣度及黏著度有所提升，預估未來 1~2 年仍有營收 25%以上之成長機會。水冷零組
件在策略投資人加入後能有更明確的產能規劃，預估在 2026~2027 年分別有 5% / 
12%的營收佔比，帶動產品組合轉佳。人型機器人則帶來潛在的想像成長空間。研究
員預估 2026 年/2027 年營收 分別為 105.18 億元/135.93 億元，YoY+36.61% / 
29.23%。EPS 2.74 元/5.74 元，YoY+86.54% / 196.64%。過去車用零組件相關公司
評價約落在 10X~20X 區間 。考量電動車市場仍在擴張 ， 且有潛在新業務將於2026 年
開始貢獻營收，維持買進投資建議，目標價 110 元(2027F PE 19x)。

中磊 (5388 TT) 
營收動能強

永豐觀點 
訂單滿手，最晚 3Q26 營收將創歷史新高。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持中磊買進建議：中磊 1Q26 財報不如預期，但 2Q26、3Q26 營收有機
會連續創歷史新高，目前已經在談 1H27 訂單，也已經在做相關訂單的記憶體備
貨，DAA 新品貢獻則有些遞延，2027 年才會更明顯，預估 2026 年 EPS 7.33 元，
目標價因 2026/2027 獲利調升，上調至 110 元(15X 2026 EPS)。

### Page 2

2

中磊為電信網通設備專業製造廠：1Q26 各產品營收占比分別為 Broadband CPE 
59％，IoT & Infra 21％，Enterprise 20％。主要客戶為歐美龍頭電信運營商與有
線頻道業者及系統整合商，中磊的主要競爭力在於軟韌體與硬體協同開發設計能
力、客製化服務能力，主要競爭對手為國內網通業者啟碁 (6285 TT) 與智易(3596 
TT)等。 
1Q26 財報不如預期：中磊 1Q26 EPS 1.01 元，不如預期，本業獲利略優於預期，
但受業外拖累影響稅後淨利表現，存貨水位由 4Q25 的 194.65 億元增至 229.32
億元，DOI 也從 122 天增加至 134 天，主要為了未來幾季的訂單備料。 
2Q26 營收有機會創歷史新高：中磊 2Q26 既有 cable 和企業網通訂單滿手，搭配
記憶體成本轉嫁，2Q26 營收有機會創歷史新高，3Q26 營收將繼續季增，但毛利
率受成本增加影響預計增幅相對有限，整體預估 2Q26 營收為 186.95 億元
(+12.3%QoQ，+53.1%YoY)，毛利率 13.6%，營業利益率 3.6%，稅後淨利 5.41
億元(+79.5%QoQ，+61.9%YoY)，稅後 EPS 1.81 元。 
訂單滿手 ， 記憶體已備到1H27：公司目前已經在談 1H27 訂單 ， 因應記憶體缺料 ，
也提前備貨 1H27 需要的記憶體 ，2026 年公司重要營運動能來自高毛利率的 DAA
產品大量出貨，且十分仰賴該產品線讓毛利率回升，但目前 DAA 產品出貨進度有
遞延，原預計2026 年有望佔營收 10%，目前研究部預估占比下修至 5%內，2027

年才會更明顯放量，整體預估 2026 年營收為 749.83 億元(+38.9%YoY)，毛利率

1 
6257 矽格(SIGURD)

報告日期： 2026/06/24  研 究 員：翁浩軒 (Davy Wong) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一. AI、高速運算帶動測試供不應求 
AI 應用帶動 AI 、 ASIC 晶片需求爆發 ， 因 AI 及 HPC 晶片複雜度高 、
製程及封裝成本大幅提升，對於高階測試需求亦放量，除了晶片
測試時間增加，部分晶片亦增加 SLT 測試、Burn-In 測試，使半導
體測試產能呈現供不應求狀況，測試價格亦提升。公司積極擴充
產能，湖口二廠約 8500 坪，2026 年下半年開始量產，新增產能已
獲國外矽光子客戶包下;中興三廠約 11000 坪，預計提前於 2026 年
第四季完工，並於 2027 年第一季進入量產。

二、2Q26 營收可望雙位數季增，預估 EPS 2.45 元 
矽格 2Q26 受惠於客戶在 AI 伺服器、ASIC、AI Connectivity、矽光
子、網通晶片及記憶體等領域需求持續擴增，稼動率維持高檔，
營收預期將續創新高，預估矽格 2Q26 營收 59.18 億(QoQ+11.8%、
YoY+23.85%)，稅後淨利 11.84 億(QoQ+17.72%、YoY+252.71%)，
EPS 2.45 元。

三.  2026 年獲利雙位數成長，預估 EPS 10.42 元 
展望 2026 年，AI 伺服器、ASIC、AI Connectivity 、矽光子、網通
晶片、低軌衛星、記憶體及矽電容等應用領域之市場需求強勁，
公司稼動率維持高檔，公司今明年亦分別有湖口二廠及中興三廠
加入營運貢獻，將帶動公司營收逐季成長。預估矽格 2026 年營收
244.16 億(YoY+24.65%) ， 稅後淨利 50.41 億(YoY+70.62%) ， EPS 10.42
元。預估 2027 年營收 322.40 億(YoY+32.05%)，稅後淨利 72.73 億
(YoY+44.27%)，EPS 15.03 元。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 300 元 
投資建議方面，矽格為專業績優封測廠，受惠於 AI ASIC 及矽光
子客戶需求強勁，營收逐季走高，下半年在新產能貢獻下營收將
持續創高。考量到 1)AI 趨勢帶動 ASIC、光通訊需求強勁，高階產
品測試需求放量 、2)封測產業供不應求 ， 測試業漲價有助毛利率提
升、3)今明年營收獲利雙位數成長，參考 2027 年 EPS 給予 20 倍本
益比，目標價 300 元，建議買進。

2 
貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
矽格成立於 1988 年 12 月 ， 總部位於新竹竹東 ， 主要從事封測業務 ，
為台灣最大 RF 測試廠之一，測試業務營收近 8 成，產品之終端應
用包括智慧型手機、車用及醫療、網通及物聯網、消費電子及智
慧家庭、電腦及週邊等; 另外 2 成為封裝業務，提供 PC、電源管
理 IC 等 Turnkey Services 為主。2017 年 7 月，公司入股台星科，成
為大股東，持股 52%。2021 年 1 月下旬宣佈收購新加坡封測大廠
聯測子公司台灣聯測的全部股權，台灣聯測成為其子公司，並取
得聯測位於竹科的總部廠房。矽格主要客戶為國內外 IC 設計廠，
競爭對手為國內外封測大廠。2026 年 1~5 月依應用別營收比重：
智慧型手機 31%、車用及醫療 6%、網通/物聯網 22%、消費電子/
智慧家庭 18%、AI/ASIC/矽光子/高速運算 23%。

二.2026 年營運概況及展望  
1. AI、高速運算帶動測試供不應求 
AI 應用帶動 AI 、 ASIC 晶片需求爆發 ， 因 AI 及 HPC 晶片複雜度高 、
製程及封裝成本大幅提升，對於高階測試需求亦放量，除了晶片
測試時間增加，部分晶片亦增加 SLT 測試、Burn-In 測試，使半導
體測試產能呈現供不應求狀況，測試價格亦提升。矽格憑藉其多
年測試經驗，提供 AI 、手機、車用、網通、消費性電子晶片測試，
2026 年前 5 月除了 2 月因工作天數較少影響，其餘月份營收均逐
月創高 。 因應測試需求強勁 ， 公司積極擴充產能 ， 湖口二廠約 8500
坪，2026 年下半年開始量產，新增產能已獲國外矽光子客戶包下;
中興三廠約 11000 坪，預計提前於 2026 年第四季完工，並於 2027
年第一季進入量產。公司 2026 年 5 月追加資本支出 28.22 億元，
使全年資本支出達 88 億元，較原規劃增加約 48%，以因應客戶在
AI 伺服器、ASIC、AI Connectivity、矽光子、網通晶片及矽電容等
領域測試需求持續提升。

資料來源：矽格

2. 1Q26 營收創單季新高，EPS 2.08 元 
矽格 1Q26 營收 52.94 億(QoQ+0.46%、YoY+13.0%)，儘管 1Q 為傳
統淡季，工作天數較少，但在需求強勁加上高階測試比重提升，
單季營收仍創新高，在營收規模放大加上折舊金額下降下，毛利
率升至 31.66%，稅後淨利 10.06 億(QoQ-4.94%、YoY+32.78%)，EPS 
2.08 元。

3. 2Q26 營收可望雙位數季增，預估 EPS 2.45 元 
矽格 2Q26 將受惠於客戶在 AI 伺服器、ASIC、AI Connectivity、矽
光子、網通晶片及記憶體等領域需求持續擴增，稼動率維持高檔，
營收預期將續創新高，5 月營收 1 9 . 7 6 億( M o M + 1 . 5 8 %、
YoY+23.71%) ， 預 估 矽 格 2Q26 營 收 59.18 億 (QoQ+11.8% 、
YoY+23.85%) ，預期毛利率在稼動率維持高檔及折舊控管下提升至
33.7%，稅後淨利 11.84 億(QoQ+17.72%、YoY+252.71%)，EPS 2.45
元。

4. 2026 年獲利雙位數成長，預估 EPS 10.42 元 
展望 2026 年，AI 伺服器、ASIC、AI Connectivity 、矽光子、網通
晶片、低軌衛星、記憶體及矽電容等應用領域之市場需求強勁，
且因應高階晶片測試需求升級，公司持續強化高速高頻、高腳數
與高散熱晶片測試技術，克服高頻、高複雜度及長測試時間等挑
戰，提升先進晶片測試服務能力。預期 AI 帶動之測試需求強勁，
公司稼動率維持高檔，公司今明年亦分別有湖口二廠及中興三廠
加入營運貢獻，一個新廠滿載可提升公司產能 10~20%，預期將帶
動公司營收逐季成長。預估矽格 2 0 2 6 年營收 2 4 4 . 1 6 億
(YoY+24.65%)，毛利率受惠於高階產品比重增加、稼動率提升、
成本控管、生產自動化帶動而提升至 33.4%，稅後淨利 50.41 億
(YoY+70.62%) ， EPS 10.42 元 。 預 估 2027 年 營 收 322.40 億
( Y o Y + 3 2 . 0 5 % )，毛利率上升至 3 5 . 0 7 %，稅後淨利 7 2 . 7 3 億
(YoY+44.27%)，EPS 15.03 元。

美國 PV 用藥人數成長強勁，1Q26 EPS 優於市場預期 27% 
藥華藥 1Q26 營收 51.21 億元，QoQ+4.9%/YoY+57.2%，符合市場預期，
受惠於美國、日本行銷團隊擴編效益顯現；稅後淨利優於元大 /市場預期
77%/27%，1Q26調整後 EPS為 5.79元。展望後市，預期 2Q26/3Q26底
美國用藥人數 QoQ+9%/+9%，帶動 2Q26/3Q26 合併營收達 69.71 億元
/71.96億元，QoQ+36%/+3%(上調 48%/34%)，2Q26/3Q26 EPS預估上調
56%/33%至 6.97元/7.23元，QoQ+20%/+4%。 
3Q26 有望取得美國 ET 藥證，挹注下一成長動能 
藥華藥以 SURPASS-ET 臨床三期試驗(二線治療)數據為基礎申請各國藥證，
並以 EXCEED-ET作為確認性證據，自 2025年 12月 29日起，美國 ET藥
證申請進入實質審查階段，FDA 審查目標完成日期(PDUFA date)為 2026年
8月 30日，基於臨床二/三期正向數據，公司將爭取 Ropeg作為 ET全線療
法核准。預估美國平均 ET 用藥人數從 2026 年約 244 人逐年成長 600-
1,000人，美國 ET病患人數達 14.8萬人，由於有效且副作用低的 ET核准
藥物仍稀缺，預期 Ropeg用於治療 ET於 2036年銷售高峰可達 19.9億美元。 
Ropeg 坐穩 MPN 治療領導地位，預估 2027 年 EPS 達 41.7 元 
展望 2027年，Ropeg在美國低風險及高風險 PV病患的滲透率持續提升，
加上新適應症原發性血小板增生症 (ET)核准，使 Ropeg 在美國目標病患族
群翻倍，預估 2027 年年底 Ropeg 美國用藥人數將成長至 6,774 人
(YoY+33%)，帶動 2027 年合併營收達 379.55 億元(YoY+34%)，毛利率
91.2% (YoY+0.7pp t)，預估 2027年 EPS為 41.69元(YoY+35%)。藥華藥
獲利成長趨勢明確，將目標本益比從前次的 25倍上調至 27倍，改以 2027
年 EPS 41.69 元，推得新目標價 1,125元，目前評價合理，維持「持有-超
越同業」評等，建議待股價修正後再留意長期買點。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
美國 PV 用 藥 人 數 成 長 強 勁 ，1Q26 EPS 優 於 市 場 預 期 27% ；
2Q26/3Q26 EPS 預估上調 56%/33%至 6.97 元/7.23 元。 
⧫  
3Q26 有望取得美國 ET 藥證，預估美國平均 ET 用藥人數從 2026 年約
244 人逐年成長 600-1,000 人，挹注下一成長動能。 
⧫  
Ropeg 坐穩 MPN 治療領導地位，預估 2027 年 EPS 41.7 元(YoY 
+35%)，上調目標價至 1,125 元(27x 2027F EPS)，維持持有評等。

營運分析  
美國用藥人數成長強勁，1Q26 EPS 優於市場預期 27% 
藥華藥 1Q26營收 51.21億元，QoQ+4.9%/YoY+57.2%，符合市場預期，營收成長係受惠於 1) 美
國、日本行銷團隊擴編效益顯現，讓 Ropeg在美國市場滲透率提升；及 2) 日本市場採用高劑量給
藥方案，帶動日本用藥人數成長。 1Q26 毛利率 87.9%，QoQ-1.0ppt/YoY-1.0ppt，低於元大/市場
預期 0.9ppt/1.2ppt，主要受產品組合與季節性因素影響，毛利率 1~2%波動為正常區間；行銷團隊
擴編使營業費用年增 34.8%至 24.1億元，營業利益 20.87億元，QoQ+33.7%/YoY+88.8%，優於
元大/市場預期 49%/8%；營業利益率達 40.8%(QoQ+8.8ppt/YoY+6.8ppt)，創單季歷史新高，優於
元大/市場預期 5.1ppt/2.7ppt；業外收入 2.49億元，主要來自利息收入及外幣兌換利益，稅前淨利
23.36億元，QoQ+16%/YoY+60%，優於元大/市場預期 53.7%/7.6%；所得稅率 8%較低，歸屬母
公司稅後淨利為 21.5 億元，QoQ+45.6%/YoY+70.1%，優於元大/市場預期 76.8%/27%，調整後
EPS為 5.79元。

用藥人數成長優於預期，加上藥價提高，預期 2Q26/3Q26 營收 QoQ+36%/+3% 
藥華藥 2026 年 5 月營收達 24.6 億元，MoM+19.1%/YoY+108.5%，4-5 月累計營收達成前次
2Q26營收預估 96%，遠優於預期，主因 1) Ropeg 在全球市場需求強勁；及 2) CMS 於 3/26將藥
華藥狀態由「特定製造商」改為「特定小型製造商」，並追溯自 2025年 1月 1日生效，藥華藥可
享有之美國藥價折扣相關優惠，公司依會計準則於 5月營收中認列部分相關調整金額，若排除此影
響，5月營收仍較 4月成長，顯示核心產品銷售規模持續放大。藥華藥目前全球員工人數 700~800
人，預計 2026 年底全球員工人數將擴編至 1,000 人以上，隨行銷團隊擴編效益顯現，預期
2Q26/3Q26底美國 Ropeg用藥人數 QoQ+9%/+9%至 3,851人/4,201人，加上出廠價提升，帶動
2Q26/3Q26 合併營收達 69.71 億元 /71.96 億元， QoQ+36%/+3%、YoY+94%/+85%(分別上修
48%/34%)。預估 2Q26/3Q26 EPS 6.97 元/7.23 元，QoQ+20%/+4%、YoY+210%/+82%，上調
56%/33%，主因 Ropeg營收展望優於預期，且費用管控得宜。

Besremi 已成為治療指南唯一同時在低風險及高風險 PV 病患治療的首選藥物 
藥華藥在美國 PV新藥行銷佈局上，得益於 1) 新藥保險覆蓋充足；2) 每年藥價穩定調漲，2026年
年初以來藥價上漲 13.4%至目前的一針 10,421 美元(2026/5/25 起)；及 3) 根據 NCCN 指引，
Ropeg已成為唯一同時在低風險及高風險 PV病患治療的首選藥物。相比 2023年，2024-2026年
美國 NCCN指引持續將 Besremi (Ropeginterferon alfa -2b-njft)列為低風險/高風險真性紅血球增生
症(PV)病患在細胞減滅療法的首選藥物，但將仿單外使用(off-label use)的競爭干擾素藥物 Pegasys 
(Peginterferon alfa -2a)的推薦順序往後，這個變動將降低保險公司給付 Pegasys用於治療 PV病患
的意願，過去美國有約 1.3萬名 PV病患使用 Pegasys治療，這些病患若沒有保險給付，可能會促
使醫生改為開立 Besremi處方箋。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：生技製藥 2026年 6月 23日 PharmaEssentia 第4 頁，共 21頁  
圖 4：Besremi 美國處方箋數量逐季增長(左圖)；Besremi 美國藥價走勢(右圖)

資料來源：Symphony Health、元大投顧整理 
Pegasys主要用於 C型肝炎與 B型肝炎治療，於 2024年 8月獲得歐盟核准用於治療真性紅血球增
生症(PV)及血小板增生症(ET)，在此之前，Pegasys 在歐美已經透過仿單外使用治療 PV及 ET病
患多年。Pegasys唯一的原料藥製造商 Roche於 2017年生產最後一批 Pegasys原料藥後便停止
生產，並於 2021 年將 Pegasys 除了中國及日本地區以外的全球商業化權利賣給 pharmaand 
(pharma&)公司，雖然當初 Roche 預計可以滿足十年的需求，但近期市場對於 Pegasys 的需求增
加，且 Pegylation 生產有難度，現在全球用藥需求持續消化 Pegasys 庫存，優先供應美國市場。
2024年歐洲、加拿大已有 Pegasys缺藥問題，美國 FDA於 2025年 1月 24日公告 pharma&公司
的 Pegasys斷貨，Pegasys 180μg/1ml劑型最近預計 2027年第一季以前才有可能重新供貨美國市
場；澳洲藥物管理局(TGA)預計 Pegasys 預填充注射器(135μg/0.5ml 及 180μg/0.5ml)短缺將延續
至 2026年 9月。Pegasys 180μg/1ml(小玻璃瓶裝)劑型在 MPN治療領域重要性高，主因為了降低
干擾素的骨髓抑制、肝毒性或精神副作用，起始劑量通常從 45μg 或 90μg 開始往上緩慢滴定，
Pegasys 180μg/1ml(小玻璃瓶裝)劑型的缺乏對於尚未滴定至最高劑量 180μg的病患在取得藥物上
有受限。 
Pegasys經過質譜儀分析會出現 8個峰值(peak)，顯示藥物包含超過 8種同分異構物(isomer)，藥
品成分複雜(圖 5)，pharma&透過新產線生產，要複製過去 Roche生產的 Pegasys藥物成分有難度，
故 pharma&在進行生產基地轉移、製程與生物製造產能擴充時遇到了技術與排程的銜接挑戰，目
前 Pegasys新產線尚未通過美國 FDA的查核。 
Besremi是第三代 Pegylated干擾素 α，可在特定單一位點做 Pegylation修飾，經過質譜儀分析僅
有一個 peak，顯示 Besremi 藥物成分單純，副作用低，最高用藥劑量可達 540μg，遠高於
Pegasys的 180μg，故 Besremi在臨床上應用更廣、療效更佳，且副作用較少。在 Pegasys全球
缺藥問題蔓延下，Besremi在美國及美國以外地區的市佔率有望進一步提升。 
圖 5：Besremi 在質譜儀分析下出現單一峰值，成分單純；Pegasys 有 8 個峰值，成分複雜

資料來源：公司資料

過去美國有約 1.3萬名 PV病患使用 Pegasys治療，2025年 NCCN最新治療指引，在 Pegasys短
缺的情況下，Ropeg (Besremi)在 PV 治療建議中，正式被列為首選替代療法，可能會促使醫生改
為開立 Besremi處方箋。即便競爭藥品 Pegasys缺貨問題延續，藥華藥管理層表示近期 Ropeg在
美國滲透率提升主要得益於 NCCN治療指引的推薦，以及在低風險 PV病患治療的推廣有成。 
藥華藥專注於說服醫生在低風險 PV病患使用 Besremi (Ropeg)，基於以下臨床效益及優勢：1) 與
最佳可用治療相比，使用 Ropeg治療 PV的無事件存活期超過 7.5年(圖 6)；2) 在 Peginvera study
中顯示，使用 Ropeg治療的 72個月內，可顯著降低 JAK2V617F等位基因負擔中位數(圖 7)，顯
示 Ropeg具有修復基因突變，並阻止疾病持續惡化的潛力；且 3) Ropeg 是美國 NCCN治療指南
中在低風險 PV 病患細胞減滅療法唯一被推薦的首選藥物。拓展低風險 PV治療市場，不僅為公司
讓目標 PV 病患人數增加 2-3 萬人至 12-13 萬人左右，也有助於延後美國 PV 病患使用 HU、
Pegasys、Jakafi 等競爭藥物的時間，不過現階段公司將專注於增加病患繼續用藥的比例 ，根據美
國商業保險資料庫分析顯示，美國新用藥的 PV病患採用 Ropeg的比例已提升至 2成。除了 PV患
者用藥人數持續成長，隨 2026 年 8 月底即將取得美國 ET 藥證，將使目標病患族群翻倍，預估
2026/2027 年美國 Besremi 用藥人數新增 約 1,887 人 /1,673 人至 5,101 人 /6,774 人
(YoY+59%/+33%)，並貢獻藥華藥 2026/2027年營收 236.75億元/337.54億元，YoY+92%/+43%。

Besremi 在日本單次處方量提升使病患負擔下降，日本市場成為營運第二隻腳 
藥華藥於 1Q23底取得 Besremi日本 PV二線療法藥證，2024/6/1起在日本新藥處方限制從過去以
14 天為限，放寬至一次可以拿三個月藥量 (針對已經打到最高劑量 500μg/針的病患)，有效減少患
者回院就診次數，並降低醫療費用負擔，Besremi在日本健保核價為一針 518,154日幣(500μg/1ml
劑型)，病患每兩個禮拜用藥一次，以自付額 30%計算，一個月應付自付額達 31萬日幣以上，該金
額遠高於日本最高所得每月自付額上限 (27.03 萬日幣以上)，若可以一次領三個月的藥量，則一年
病患自付總額只要原來的三分之一，有望提升病患採用意願。日本 PV病患對於 Besremi接受度高，
目前不繼續用藥的比例只有 3%，藥華藥近年積極在各國推廣“250μg→350μg→500μg”的「HIDAT
劑量快速爬升」給藥方案，讓病患在最短時間內(24 週內)達到完全血液學反應(CHR)，病患越快看
到療效，對藥物順從性 (Compliance)就越高，能大幅降低因前期療效不彰而中途停藥 (Drop-out)的
機率。本中心預估 2026/2027年日本 Besremi平均用藥人數約 760人/1,150人，每人每年出廠價
約 10萬美元，則預估 2026/2027年 Besremi日本市場營收貢獻 7,600萬美元/1.15億美元。

與歐洲授權夥伴 AOP 間仲裁案懸而未決 
藥華藥與歐洲授權夥伴 AOP之間於 2018-2022年間之第一仲裁案落幕，德國最高法院裁決，授權
合約有效，且藥華藥不須支付賠償金及利息費用。不過藥華藥主張 AOP 違約(就 AOP 公司延遲交
付臨床試驗資料導致藥華藥申請美國藥證延遲，及 AOP 公司違反授權合約未啟動其他三項適應症
臨床試驗)，於 2020年下半年向國際商會國際仲裁院(ICC)提請第二仲裁案，請求 AOP賠償 20多
億歐元，而 AOP在該仲裁案中反過來向藥華藥求償 60億歐元。由於第二仲裁案正在進行，目前藥
華藥仍未開始認列歐洲銷售權利金分潤，藥華藥已於 2023 年底遞交資料給仲裁庭，2025/2/17 藥
華藥經由委任律師接獲國際商會(ICC)部分仲裁判決(Partial Arbitration Final Award) ，包括 1) 關於
雙方主張對方延遲藥證申請之損害賠償部分，仲裁庭駁回藥華藥的主張，而保留 AOP 的主張，藥
華藥須負賠償責任，但賠償金仍未定； 2) 關於雙方在產品價格的主張皆予以保留； 3) 關於藥華藥
主張 AOP 須依照合約繳交貨款、給付銷售權利金 (royalty)的主張予以保留；4) 由於臨床藥物價格
調整，藥華藥須支付 AOP 20.9 萬歐元(須支付利息費用)；及 5) 針對前次仲裁案費用，藥華藥須支
付 135萬歐元費用(須支付利息費用)。整體來看，藥華藥至少須支出約 156 萬歐元(約 5,400 多萬
元台幣)，因延誤歐盟藥證申請 5個月及延遲供貨 4.5個月(合計 9.5個月)而須付給 AOP的賠償金
額未定。授權合約依然有效，藥華藥持續供應 Besremi給 AOP，待仲裁案落幕後可向 AOP收取貨
款及銷售權利金分潤，出廠價仍待協商。藥華藥已於 2026 年 4 月底向德國聯邦最高法院(BGH)上
訴撤銷部分仲裁判斷的聲請，該撤仲主要範圍包含仲裁庭認定藥華藥對 AOP 歐洲藥證及供貨遲延
9.5個月之責任；本次裁定僅涉及「部分仲裁判斷」之撤銷程序，並非最終判決。  
其他市場開發部分，Besremi分別於 2020/6、2021/10取得台灣、韓國的 PV藥證，台灣於 2022/9
通過健保核價為一年新台幣 286萬元(相當於美國藥價的<50%)，2025年 8月獲得韓國納入健保； 
2024年陸續獲得新加坡、馬來西亞、中國的 PV藥證，並在 4Q25取得香港 PV藥證。關於中國市
場拓展，Besremi 於 2024/6/28 取得中國藥證，藥華藥短期將以自費方式拓展中國市場，鎖定 30
幾家重點醫院，也會找適合的合作夥伴一起經營中國市場。 Besremi 至今已獲得全球逾 50 個國家
核准用於治療成人真性紅血球增生症 (PV)患者，包括美國、日本、中國及歐盟等主要新藥市場。此
外，公司於 2025年 10月向美國 FDA遞交 Ropeg筆型注射劑藥證申請，有望於 2026年獲准上市，
管理層預期 Ropeg筆型注射劑單價將高於現行針劑，與保險公司洽談藥價中。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：生技製藥 2026年 6月 23日 PharmaEssentia 第8 頁，共 21頁
ET 臨床三期數據正向，預計 3Q26 取得美國藥證，為下一成長動能
P1101 用於治療原發性血小板增生症(ET)已通過美國罕見疾病用藥認證。SURPASS-ET 是一項開
放性、多中心、隨機分配、有效藥物對照之多國多中心臨床三期試驗以比較 P1101 與 Anagrelide
(已核准上市的 ET 二線療法)做為二線治療對於原發性血小板增多症 (ET)之療效、安全性與耐受性
並評估藥物動力學，在中國、美國、韓國、日本、台灣、香港、新加坡與加拿大共收案 174人，其
中 91 名隨機分配至 P1101 組，83 名隨機分配至 Anagrelide (ANA)組。療效反應(Modified ELN
response)係定義為受試者治療後必須以下四項條件均達標： 1) 周邊血球計數減少 (血小板
≤400x109/L 及白血球 <9.5x109/L)；2) 疾病相關的體徵改善或未惡化 (脾臟腫大 )；3) 依據 MPN-

SAF-TSS評分具有較大的症狀改善或保持無疾病進展；及 4) 未發生出血與血栓事件。
2025/1/4公佈 SURPASS-ET臨床試驗數據，主要療效評估指標顯示，P1101組有 42.9%受試者在
第 9 個月及第 12 個月均有療效反應(Modified ELN response) ，顯著高於對照組 Anagrelide 組的
6%，療效達到統計上顯著差異(p=0.0001)，顯示 P1101 長期的療效優於 Anagrelide；次要療效指
標顯示，P1101組的 JAK-2等位基因負擔平均值從基礎線(baseline)的 33.65%下降至第 12個月的
25.26%，相比同期 Anagrelide組的 JAK-2等位基因負擔平均值從 39.71%下降至 37.26%，P1101
更能有效降低 JAK-2基因突變的負擔，有助於降低 MPN疾病的嚴重程度和惡化風險；P1101組受
試者整體安全性與耐受度良好，在整體數值上顯示較 Anagrelide 組相對良好。藥華藥將以
SURPASS-ET 臨床三期試驗 (二線治療)數據為基礎申請各國藥證，目前已向中國、台灣、日本、

美國藥監機構遞交 ET藥證申請，有望於 2026年 8月底取得美國 ET藥證。
由於 SURPASS-ET的美國受試者太少，公司也執行 EXCEED-ET臨床二期試驗(僅收治美國 ET病
患、全線療法)，已於 2024年 3月完成 91名美國 ET病患收案，可作為美國 ET藥證審核的輔助資
料，如果安全性、有效性良好，不排除跟 FDA 溝通仿單是否可以作為 ET 全線用藥。P1101 作為
ET第二線用藥已於 PV三期臨床試驗中取得概念性驗證，研判 P1101可於 2026年取得美國 ET藥
證，成為下一成長動能。
藥華藥有望於 2026年 8月底取得美國 ET藥證，預估美國平均 ET用藥人數從 2026年約 244人逐
年成長 600-1,000人，美國 ET病患人數達 14.8萬人(其中約 65%帶有 JAK2V617F基因突變)，由
於有效且副作用低的 ET核准藥物仍稀缺，我們認為 Besremi在美國 ET治療市場的市佔率有望達
10%，即用藥人數達 1萬人以上，出廠價為每人每年 18.9萬美元，則 P1101作為治療 ET第二線

用藥於 2036年銷售高峰可達 19.9億美元。另一方面，可關注公司在更多癌症的新藥開發，包括 1)
Ropeg 用於 PMF 治療臨床三期(HOPE-PMF)已完成收案，預計 2028 年取得數據後遞交美國藥證
申請，以及 2) 藥華藥正在進行 P1101合併 anti-PD-1抗體用於治療肝癌之一期臨床試驗。
